# Global data of the r0001 image: `0xADDR SIZE name  # note`, the format of symbols.txt.
# Functions and data share one namespace (the differ resolves either name to an address);
# the rows are kept apart so every tool that walks the function map stays a function walk.
# A placeholder `data_<VA>` needs no row -- the differ reads its address out of the name --
# but a row may carry what is known about it.
0x00100000 0x0 mfifoBase  # MAIN; SOCOM 1's local symbol at 0x100000 (the MFIFO ring's base: zSysFifoInit and zSysFifoEnable store it in D_RBOR, D8_MADR and the drain's TADR, as SOCOM 1's relocations name mfifoBase); 4 references in 2 functions -- the sound/system lane
